This establishes the inequality (
229
).

F.4
Proof of the claims (
45a
) and (
45b
)

We are now ready to justify the claims (
45a
) and (
45b
).
Combining (
195
) and (
219
), we reach

\displaystyle\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s}))}(s,a_{1})

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s}))}(s,a_{1}),&\text{if }t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s})\leq t\leq t_{\mathsf{tran}}\\
\Big(\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{tran}})}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s}))}(s,a_{1})\Big)+\Big(\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{tran}})}(s,a_{1})\Big),&\text{if }t_{\mathsf{tran}}\leq t\leq t_{\mathsf{ref}}\end{cases}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s})\leq i\leq t_{\mathsf{tran}}}\Big(\theta^{(i)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s}))}(s,a_{1})\Big)+\max_{t_{\mathsf{tran}}\leq i<t_{\mathsf{ref}}}\Big(\theta^{(i)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{tran}})}(s,a_{1})\Big)\leq 1.

This taken collectively with (
188
) leads to

\displaystyle\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{1})

\displaystyle\leq\theta^{(t_{\overline{s-1}}(\tau_{s}))}(s,a_{1})+1\leq-\frac{1}{2}\log\left(1+\frac{c_{\mathrm{m}}\gamma}{35}\eta(1-\gamma)t_{s-2}(\tau_{s-2})\right)+1,

as claimed in (
45b
).

In addition, recalling the definition (
185
) of
t_{\mathsf{ref}}
, we have

\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{0})\leq\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{1})-\log\big(c_{\mathsf{ref}}(1-\gamma)\big),

which clearly satisfies (
45a
) as long as
c_{\mathsf{ref}}\geq c_{\mathrm{p}}/16128
.

F.5
Proof of the claim (
45c
)

Finally, we move on to analyze what happens after iteration
t_{\mathsf{ref}}
, for which we focus on
tracking the changes of
\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)
.
In this part, let us only consider the set of
t
satisfying

\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\leq\pi^{(t)}(a_{2}\,|\,s).

Note that at time
t_{\mathsf{ref}}
, the inequalities (
45a
) and (
45b
)
are both satisfied, which together with the property
\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\leq\pi^{(t)}(a_{2}\,|\,s)
yield

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\coloneqq\exp\Big(\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-\max_{a}\theta^{(t)}(s,a)\Big)=\exp\Big(\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{2})\Big).

Then, if
c_{\mathsf{ref}}<c_{\mathrm{p}}/1000
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(t+1)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)-\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)=

\displaystyle~\exp\Big(\theta^{(t+1)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t+1)}(s,a_{2})\Big)-\exp\Big(\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{2})\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\Big\{\exp\Big(\theta^{(t+1)}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t+1)}(s,a_{2})-\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{1})+\theta^{(t)}(s,a_{2})\Big)-1\Big\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle~\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\max\Big\{\exp\Big(\eta\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{1})}-\eta\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{2})}\Big)-1,~0\Big\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\cdot 2\eta\max\left\{\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{1})}-\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{2})},~0\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~56c_{\mathrm{m}}\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}\Big[\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\Big]^{2}.

(233)

Here, the first inequality holds if
\eta\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{1})}-\eta\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{2})}\leq 1
(given the elementary fact
e^{x}-1\leq 2x
for any
0\leq x\leq 1
), and the last line is valid since

\displaystyle\frac{\partial V^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{1})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}d_{\mu}^{(t)}(s)\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\Big(Q^{(t)}(s,a_{1})-V^{(t)}(s)\Big)\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}14c_{\mathrm{m}}(1-\gamma)\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s),

\displaystyle\frac{\partial V^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{2})}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}d_{\mu}^{(t)}(s)\left\{\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\Big(Q^{(t)}(s,a_{2})-Q^{(t)}(s,a_{1})\Big)+\pi^{(t)}(a_{0}\,|\,s)\Big(Q^{(t)}(s,a_{2})-Q^{(t)}(s,a_{0})\Big)\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\geq}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}d_{\mu}^{(t)}(s)\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\Big(Q^{(t)}(s,a_{2})-Q^{(t)}(s,a_{1})\Big)\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\geq}-14c_{\mathrm{m}}(1-\gamma)\pi^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s),

where (ii) holds since
Q^{(t)}(s,a_{2})\geq Q^{(t)}(s,a_{0})
(cf. (
208
)),
and (i) and (iii) make use of Lemma
1
and Lemma
3
.
In addition, these bounds also imply that
\eta\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{1})}-\eta\frac{\partial{V}^{(t)}(\mu)}{\partial\theta(s,a_{2})}\leq 1
hold as long as
28\eta c_{\mathrm{m}}(1-\gamma)\leq 1
, thus validating the argument for the first inequality in (
233
).

Armed with the above recursive relation (
233
),
we can invoke Lemma
11
to show that

\displaystyle t_{s}({\tau}_{s})-t_{\mathsf{ref}}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(a_{1}\,|\,s)}-\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}^{(t_{s}({\tau}_{s}))}(a_{1}\,|\,s)}}{56c_{\mathrm{m}}\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}}\geq\frac{\frac{1}{\widehat{\pi}^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(a_{1}\,|\,s)}-\frac{2}{1-\gamma}}{56c_{\mathrm{m}}\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}},

(235)

where the last inequality holds since (in view of (
132
) and (
60
)).

\widehat{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\geq{\pi}^{(t)}(a_{1}\,|\,s)\geq(1-\gamma)/2\qquad\text{for any }t\geq t_{s}(\tau_{s}).

In order to control
t_{s}({\tau}_{s})-t_{\mathsf{ref}}
via (
235
), it remains to upper bound
\widehat{\pi}^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(a_{1}\,|\,s)
.
Towards this end, it is seen that

\displaystyle\widehat{\pi}^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(a_{1}\,|\,s)

\displaystyle=\exp\Big(\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{1})-\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{2})\Big)=\exp\Big(2\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{1})+\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{0})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\exp\Big(3\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a_{1})-\log\Big(\frac{c_{\mathrm{p}}(1-\gamma)}{16128}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16128e^{3}}{c_{\mathrm{p}}(1-\gamma)\Big(1+\frac{c_{\mathrm{m}}\gamma}{35}\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}t_{s-2}({\tau}_{s-2})\Big)^{1.5}}\leq\frac{16128e^{3}}{c_{\mathrm{p}}(1-\gamma)\Big(\frac{c_{\mathrm{m}}\gamma}{35}\eta(1-\gamma)^{2}t_{s-2}({\tau}_{s-2})\Big)^{1.5}},

(236)

where the first line uses
\sum_{a}\theta^{(t_{\mathsf{ref}})}(s,a)=0
, the second line relies on the inequality (
45a
),
and the last one applies the inequality (
45b
).
Substitution into the relation (
235
) yields

\displaystyle t_{s}({\tau}_{s})-t_{\mathsf{ref}}\geq 10^{-10}c_{\mathrm{p}}c_{\mathrm{m}}^{0.5}\eta^{0.5}(1-\gamma)^{2}\Big(t_{s-2}({\tau}_{s-2})\Big)^{1.5},

thus establishing the advertised bound.
